# Agents · R · part 7

988 entries, red-unit to Refactorer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **red-unit**: Spawned by implement-plan-module, Stage 2, with plan steps; or by rework-module with a rework's copy steps; or by any skill or pipeline with a reproduction brief (templates/reproducing.md). Not for direct use — it needs step context only an orchestrator has. TDD Unit Red Phase step agent: writes meaningful, compiling unit tests for one target class (RED phase — tests must compile and fail at runtime). Stack-agnostic; all framework, naming, and run-command detail comes from the module conventions the orchestrator points it at. ([ds-usova/tdd-sdlc](https://github.com/ds-usova/tdd-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/red-unit.md))
- **red-usability**: Red team usability specialist — guppy swarms probe completed beads for confusing APIs, poor error messages, and accessibility gaps. ([LucasQuiles/sdlc-os](https://github.com/LucasQuiles/sdlc-os/blob/HEAD/agents/red-usability.md) · ★1)
- **red-vuln**: Vulnerability Analyst for /red-team. Performs vulnerability identification, CVE research, exploit availability assessment, attack path analysis, and risk scoring. Consumes reconnaissance findings from red-recon and produces prioritized vulnerability reports for red-exploit. Operates within analysis scope with read-only target interaction. Output follows ADR-output-path-resolution-001 (P1/P2/P3 resolution). ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/red-team/agents/red-vuln.md) · ★37 · 2 copies)

- **red-writer**: RED 단계 전담 에이전트. 새 동작의 실패 테스트와 승인된 재계획의 케이스 변경을 수행한다. 프로덕션 코드는 작성하거나 보지 않는다. &lt;example&gt; Context: oh-my-gx:gx-tdd의 phase-implement RGR 사이클 진입 user: AC-1을 RGR 사이클로 구현해줘 assistant: red-writer를 호출하여 AC-1의 실패 테스트를 작성하고 실패를 확인한 뒤 implementer로 인계 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 사용자가 RED 단계만 단독 실행 user: /oh-my-gx:gx-red 비밀번호 검증 실패 시 401 응답 assistant: red-writer가 PasswordValidatorTest.shouldReject401()을 작성하고 실패 메시지를 캡처하여 보고 &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/red-writer.md))

- **redacteur**: Rédige du contenu long-format de haut niveau en appliquant un playbook de genre, un plan, des preuves et le style maison simultanément. Déclencheurs anglais : "draft the full paper", "write the methods and results sections", "write this chapter from the outline", "rewrite this section to publication standard". &lt;example&gt; Context: La compétence rediger doit produire une analyse stratégique de 20 pages. user: "Rédige l'analyse stratégique complète à partir du plan et des sources." assistant:… ([MariusYvard/Scriptorium](https://github.com/MariusYvard/Scriptorium/blob/HEAD/scriptorium/agents/redacteur.md))

- **redacteur**: Rédacteur UX et linguiste produit, FR-first et multilingue, choix de rédaction fondés sur des preuves sourcées. - **redundant-comment-remover**: PreToolUse hook (`git push` 直前) で抽出された push 対象差分のソースコードファイル (`*.go` / `*.sh`) と lint config ファイル (`.golangci.yaml` / `.markdownlint-cli2.yaml` / `.shellcheckrc` / `.yamllint` 等) から FLM_APP_0010 §書かない対象 / FLM_GEN_0006 §lint config ファイルにはコメントを書かない に該当する冗長コメントを Edit / Write で強制削除する。 違反を「指摘」 して親セッションに修正を委ねず、 subagent 自身が削除まで完結させる specialized reviewer ([wakuwaku3/flame](https://github.com/wakuwaku3/flame/blob/HEAD/plugins/flame/agents/redundant-comment-remover.md))

- **reel-editor**: Cuts one vertical reel end to end with the resolve-reel tools. Give it one content note (or brief), the footage paths, the business repo, a work folder and a version number. It reads one prep packet, writes the cut plan, runs the gates and the Resolve build, and returns the delivered file with a short report. Use for any "edit this reel", "build vN", or batch reel job; one agent per reel. ([1610-advisory/mktkit](https://github.com/1610-advisory/mktkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-editor.md) · ★1)
- **reel-expert**: Fidelity check of a short-video script by an expert in the field of the source experiment. Use together with reel-viewer on every draft or rewrite of a reel script. ([kurbaitaev/ghost-editor](https://github.com/kurbaitaev/ghost-editor/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/reel-helper.md) · ★627 · 2 copies)
- **reel-marketing-reviewer**: 깊게 파는 릴스 기획표·원고를 마케팅 관점에서 장별로 판정한다 — 새 정보인가, 기대감을 쌓는 순서인가, 2.5초 안에 읽히는가, 과장은 없는가. FAIL 이면 굽지 않는다. reel-planner 다음, push 전에 부른다. ([nonojin99/Daily-Card-News](https://github.com/nonojin99/Daily-Card-News/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-marketing-reviewer.md))
- **reel-planner**: 깊게 파는 릴스 기획 — item-scorer 가 고른 카드 하나를 받아, 카드에 없는 '더 알고 싶게 만드는 사실'을 새로 조사하고 장별 기획표(요점 장마다 다른 축)를 만든다. 원고를 쓰기 전에 부른다. ([nonojin99/Daily-Card-News](https://github.com/nonojin99/Daily-Card-News/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-planner.md))
- **reel-producer**: Use for short-form Reel/TikTok video work — cutting/captioning talking-head Reels in Vyra, writing Reel hooks/scripts/shot-lists, planning captions + hashtags + posting calendar, and researching what's performing in your niche to adapt. Delegate here whenever the task is content/video rather than app code. Produces drafts and edits; it never posts and never touches app code. ([AidenJohnston/reel-studio](https://github.com/AidenJohnston/reel-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/reel-producer.md) · ★6)
- **reel-qa**: Checks one finished reel against the house edit rules and the business's public-safe rules, from one QA JSON and one contact sheet. Give it the mp4, its edit.json, the business's reel-style.json and the content note. Returns pass or a short list of blockers. Use after every reel-editor build; one agent per reel. ([1610-advisory/mktkit](https://github.com/1610-advisory/mktkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-qa.md) · ★1)
- **reel-reviewer**: Independent reviewer of every version of a reel before it reaches the person (the /creator-reel quality lock). Reads the review.py --stage N report, looks at all its images, compares it with the rules and with what the person already rejected, scores each axis of the stage from 1 to 10 with its proof and gives the verdict (show or fix) with the 3 worst problems. Launched after review.py --stage N, passing it only the report's path, the stage and the Montage project, never the conclusions of whoever made the version. ([CamiloRincon96/creator-os](https://github.com/CamiloRincon96/creator-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **reel-reviewer**: Reviews a rendered reel of any series of this studio (REDRAWN maps, RECEIPTS money) beat by beat (clarity for a scrolling viewer, accuracy, sync, layout and safe area, pacing, art direction) and writes a prioritised fix list with concrete episode.json changes. Use after every render of an episode. ([Michliphene/redrawn-reels](https://github.com/Michliphene/redrawn-reels/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-reviewer.md))
- **reel-reviewer**: 릴스 검수 2단계 — Actions 가 만든 릴스 MP4 의 접촉 시트(첫 2초·구간별·끝 장면)와 원고·캡션을 보고 게시 가능 여부를 판정한다. 게시 관문(publish_ig.py review 검사) 앞에서 부른다. ([nonojin99/Daily-Card-News](https://github.com/nonojin99/Daily-Card-News/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-reviewer.md))
- **reel-reviewer**: 구현된 쇼릴을 검토한다. 레이아웃 자동 검사(겹침·잘림·읽기 시간)를 돌리고, 핵심 순간의 프레임을 직접 보고, 화면 수치를 스토리보드·원자료와 대조하고, 효과음 큐가 화면 사건과 맞는지 확인해 수정 목록을 만든다. 쇼릴 하네스(showreel-studio)의 검토 단계에서 호출하며 코드는 고치지 않는다. ([YUjinEDU/showreel-studio](https://github.com/YUjinEDU/showreel-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/reel-reviewer.md))
- **reel-scriptwriter**: Escreve roteiros cronometrados de Reels e TikTok (15–45s) para o Hub do Ator com demo real da tela, texto na tela e direção de câmera. ([douglaspsadm/o-hub-do-ator-social](https://github.com/douglaspsadm/o-hub-do-ator-social/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/reel-scriptwriter.md))
- **reel-storyboarder**: 보고서·조사 결과를 모션그래픽 쇼릴(기본 발표용 2분 안팎, 요청 시 30초 요약 또는 3~5분 자막형 숏동영상) 스토리보드로 바꾼다. 검증된 사실만 골라 발표 흐름(문제→방법→발견→공백→제안→다음)에 맞춘 장면별 메시지, 화면 문구, 수치와 출처, 발표 대본, 시각 콘셉트, 타이밍, 효과음 큐를 설계한다. 쇼릴 하네스(showreel-studio)의 1단계이며, 스토리보드를 다시 짜거나 장면 구성을 바꿀 때도 호출한다. ([YUjinEDU/showreel-studio](https://github.com/YUjinEDU/showreel-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/reel-storyboarder.md))
- **reel-viewer**: Cold read of a short-video script by a typical Instagram/TikTok Reels viewer (not a programmer, outside the author's circle). Use on every draft or rewrite of a reel or explainer script before showing it to the author. Give it ONLY the script (narration lines, numbered, with the visuals in brackets) and the language to answer in; never the article or your own reasoning. ([JaviMaligno/agilabs-video-studio](https://github.com/JaviMaligno/agilabs-video-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/reel-viewer.md))
- **reel-watcher**: Visual check of a rendered reel against the author's recurring complaints, before the author sees it. Use after every render of a reel or explainer (and after every re-render), once `npm run review-frames` has written out/review/&lt;cut&gt;/. Give it the path of that folder (it holds index.md, the contact sheets and the full-size frames) and the video folder; it reads script.md and public/ itself. It returns PASS / FAIL per item with timestamps, never an opinion on the script or the angle. ([JaviMaligno/agilabs-video-studio](https://github.com/JaviMaligno/agilabs-video-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/reel-watcher.md))
- **reel-worker**: Watches ONE reel or post end to end in the background (download, Gemini or local analysis, breakdown, Telegram reply) so the main reel-agent session stays free for the next message. The main session starts one of these per reel. ([HNF-FRN/Reel-watcher-telegram-Agent](https://github.com/HNF-FRN/Reel-watcher-telegram-Agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reel-worker.md) · ★5)
- **reenactor**: Turns a bug report, red CI run, or production error into the smallest test that fails for the reported reason, plus the command that runs it and ranked suspect files and lines, before anyone writes a fix. Use when a bug can't yet be triggered on demand. Don't delegate when a failing test already exists, for a one-line typo, or for behavior that doesn't exist yet, which is repertoire:test-designer's job. ([stevekinney/repertoire](https://github.com/stevekinney/repertoire/blob/HEAD/agents/reenactor.md) · ★18)
- **reeng-backend-harmonize-agent**: Analiza todos los ficheros *_tobe.md de un directorio, detecta inconsistencias de naming entre documentos (clases, DTOs, paquetes, métodos) y garantiza que cada tabla/vista Oracle sea gestionada por un único Repository. Corrige los documentos in-place. Cada documento queda autocontenido pero con naming coherente entre todos. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-backend-harmonize-agent.md))
- **reeng-backend-screen-behavior-agent**: Genera la especificación técnica Spring Boot del comportamiento inicial de una pantalla HTML con estructura idéntica a la de los UCs, centrada en capacidades reutilizables del backend core. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-backend-screen-behavior-agent.md))
- **reeng-backend-uc-agent**: Genera la especificación técnica Spring Boot para un caso de uso concreto, modelando primero la operación del backend core y dejando fuera la composición posterior de frontend. Si el UC no requiere backend, genera igualmente una salida adaptada a frontend-only. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-backend-uc-agent.md))
- **reeng-batch-agent**: Genera la especificación técnica Spring Batch para un proceso daemon concreto, mapeando su lógica legacy a Job, Steps, ItemReader/Processor/Writer y componentes Spring Batch. Produce un fichero [PROC-CODE]_[nombre]_tobe.md. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-batch-agent.md))
- **reeng-frontend-screen-agent**: Genera la especificación técnica Angular to-be del shell de una pantalla completa (layout, rutas, componentes shared, servicios compartidos y endpoints de inicialización). Produce un fichero [PANTALLA]_screen_frontend_tobe.md. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-frontend-screen-agent.md))
- **reeng-frontend-uc-agent**: Genera la especificación técnica Angular to-be de un UC concreto, mapeando su lógica legacy y el contrato del backend tobe a componentes Angular, formularios, servicios y modelos. Produce un fichero [UC-CODE]_[nombre]_frontend_tobe.md. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-frontend-uc-agent.md))
- **reengagement-agent**: Generate a personalized re-engagement message at 30, 60, or 90 days post-delivery. The message must reference something specific about their business or the project — it cannot be a generic "checking in." ([Imad-Oute/MicroHard](https://github.com/Imad-Oute/MicroHard/blob/HEAD/src/modules/fl/7-retain/mh-fl-retain-engine/agents/reengagement-agent.md) · ★2)
- **reengineering-backend-auto-agent**: Orquesta la generación de documentación de reingeniería Spring Boot para una pantalla HTML completa o para un UC concreto, centrando el diseño en un backend core reusable y sin fijar todavía la composición de frontend. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reengineering-backend-auto-agent.md))
- **reengineering-batch-auto-agent**: Orquesta la generación de especificaciones técnicas Spring Batch para uno o todos los procesos de un daemon legacy. El usuario indica el daemon y opcionalmente el proceso concreto. Delega en reeng-batch-agent para cada proceso. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reengineering-batch-auto-agent.md))
- **reengineering-frontend-auto-agent**: Orquesta la generación de especificaciones técnicas frontend to-be (Angular 21 + PrimeNG 21) de una pantalla completa o de un UC concreto, consumiendo las specs tobe del backend ya generadas y aplicando principios SOLID/DRY con reutilización de componentes y servicios. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reengineering-frontend-auto-agent.md))
- **reenquadrador-vertical**: Especialista em transformar vídeo horizontal (16:9) em vertical 9:16 de tela cheia com o edin, decidindo cena por cena (pessoa, tela de app, texto/logo, fundo branco). Use quando Rafael pedir a versão vertical de um vídeo horizontal pronto (promo, motion, demo, aula, podcast). Gera o plano, renderiza, revisa e corrige até não ter faixa nem texto cortado. ([RafaelCoutinhoLima/editor-dinamico](https://github.com/RafaelCoutinhoLima/editor-dinamico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reenquadrador-vertical.md))

- **Refactorer**: Performs structural refactoring that preserves behavior — verified by tests before and after. Use proactively when code is correct but mis-shaped (DRY violation, deep nesting, mixed concerns), ground needs preparing before a feature, or a cross-cutting rename spans several files.
